# Graph Report - MythosMUD  (2026-09-03)

## Corpus Check
- 313 files · ~2,950,727 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 54363 nodes · 102188 edges · 2352 communities (1639 shown, 713 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5722 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 224,074 input · 10,948 output
